# ZenaQuiet-Headset Status Report — 2026-11-12

Source: live Jira, project `HS` (ZenaQuiet-Headset), snapshot `data/ZenaQuiet-Headset/2026-11-12.json` (521 issues), plus `raw/threads/` and `raw/transcripts/`. Stage windows and lead times from [[reference#Stage schedule (per product)]] and [[reference#Purchasing lead times]]. Previous report: [[ZenaQuiet-Headset/jira/2026-11-09-jira|2026-11-09]]. Rows not shown in sections 3 and 5 are in the Appendix at the end.

**Since last report:** Compared with snapshot 2026-11-09: 556 → 521 issues; Added: 5; Removed: 40; Status changes: 9; Date changes: 2; Title changes: 3; Assignee changes: 6; Links added: 0; Links removed: 0.

- **The Headset PCBs (printed circuit boards) arrived, and the first board boots.** At the stand-up the boards were in and being soldered (`transcripts/2026-11-09-standup.md · Pham Nguyen Ngoc 24:50`). Firmware opened a new bring-up Story, HS-648, with four Subtasks, HS-649 to HS-652. The Linux image now boots on the board (HS-651, Done 2026-11-11). The first power test failed: the board does not start from an external supply unless a battery is connected (HS-652). The PCB risk, HIGH last week, is closed.
- **40 tickets were deleted in a clean-up.** The Zoo Meet & ZenaAI Cloud Epic HS-380 is gone, with all its Stories and Subtasks (HS-381 to HS-417), plus HS-162 (I2S driver) and HS-167. At the stand-up, Duy Cong Nguyen said these AI-drafted tickets were never reviewed (`2026-11-09-standup.md · Duy Cong Nguyen 25:23`). Cang Le said he would re-check the priority of all old Headset tickets (`· Cang Le 49:31`). As a result, open items with no owner fell from 482 to 434.
- **Chip confirmed as NXP i.MX 95 in Jira.** HS-165 ("Confirm NXP i.MX 95 availability, document SoC selection") closed Done on 2026-11-09. The board runs on a CompuLab UCM-iMX95 module (HS-649). The parent Story HS-164 is still To Do.
- **The passive Arc work was closed.** HS-78, HS-89, HS-93 and HS-94 (titled "[TO BE REMOVED]") are Done as of 2026-11-09. HS-51 is now In Process with owner Nguyen Trung Tin. On 2026-11-10 he proposed a smaller POC scope for the Arc, which is waiting for an answer.
- **Epics renamed and given owners.** HS-1 is now "Layer 3 — Inbound TSE" (owner KhuongNguyen), and its due date moved from 2027-09-06 to 2028-02-08, which is after the MP (mass production) end. HS-2 is now "Layer 1 — Outbound(ANC)" (owner Nguyen Binh Minh). HS-4 is owned by Chico Ly. HS-3 lost its due date. HS-166 (power budget) went to Phi Tuong and HS-219 (ANC audio paths) to Nguyen Binh Minh.
- **Mechanical progress.** The headband fit Story HS-580 and the clamping-force test HS-584 closed on 2026-11-12. Earcup isolation (HS-13) started.
- **Readiness rose from 33.3% to 47.8% (11/23 POC Stories).** Three Stories closed: HS-78, HS-89 and HS-580. Two POC Stories were deleted (HS-381, HS-385) and one was added (HS-648). Two of the three closures are Arc Stories marked "[TO BE REMOVED]", so real progress is smaller than the jump suggests (inferred). Health moved from RED to AMBER because the PCB risk closed.

**Current sprint:** [POC] Sprint 4 (2026-11-12 → 2026-11-15), 26 tickets: Nguyen Trung Tin 12, Phi Tuong 5, Bui Minh Tuan (Steve .Bui) 3, Hung Pham Nguyen 2, Unassigned 2, Chico Ly 2.

Note: Jira sprint states don't match the sprint dates (3 sprints, e.g. [POC] Sprint 1, [POC] Sprint 2, [POC] Sprint 3); last and current sprint are chosen by date.

- **Most of the sprint had no owner.** 28 of the 42 tickets (4 Stories, 24 Subtasks) were unassigned, and 27 of them are still open. These include the core POC Stories HS-48 (first ANC test), HS-164 (chip selection), HS-457 (Bluetooth) and HS-118 (LED visibility). The per-person numbers therefore describe only a small part of the work.
- **Nguyen Trung Tin (mechanical) worked on the Arc.** He closed HS-86, HS-127, HS-247 and HS-301 inside the sprint dates, and HS-332, HS-333 and HS-458 outside them. The work ended with a negative result: the printed passive Arc cannot reach its 8–12 dB target (HS-79, Nguyen Trung Tin, 2026-10-27). That passive approach has now been dropped (HS-51, Nguyen Trung Tin, 2026-11-10).
- **Chico Ly (ID) finished the first appearance model.** The form model HS-318 and the full-size SLA (3D-printed resin) model HS-319 are Done.
- **What slipped.** Fast-charge validation HS-422 (Nguyen Manh Tuan), the power budget HS-166 (Phi Tuong) and LED install HS-302 (Phong.ngoc.nguyen) are still open, with no update. The chip work closed outside the sprint dates (HS-581 Huynh Minh Tuan; HS-165), but its Story HS-164 is still open. The script shows 0 tickets carried into Sprint 4 because the open ones still sit in Sprint 3 in Jira.
- **Effect on the gate.** The pace line says "on pace": 3 POC Stories closed against about 1.7 needed per sprint. Two of the three (HS-86, HS-127) are Arc Stories titled "[TO BE REMOVED]", so the underlying pace is weaker (inferred). The current sprint (Sprint 4) dates in Jira are 2026-11-12 → 2026-11-15.

### Top 3 risks

**1. First Headset board has a power fault and no electronics owner**

- **What:** The board will not start from external power without a battery, and no EE ticket covers the fix.
- **Tickets:**
  - [HS-652](https://epazz.atlassian.net/browse/HS-652) — Test hardware driver peripherals on PCB (power test FAILED, In Process)
  - [HS-648](https://epazz.atlassian.net/browse/HS-648) — [POC] Build & configure operating system, driver on PCB headset
  - [HS-219](https://epazz.atlassian.net/browse/HS-219) — ANC audio paths test, "waiting for hardware being soldered"
  - [HS-3](https://epazz.atlassian.net/browse/HS-3) — Power Supply & Battery Management Epic (no due date)
  - [HS-336](https://epazz.atlassian.net/browse/HS-336) — Provide confirmed PCB Rev 1 envelope (no owner)
- **Impact:** Firmware and ANC tests on the only POC board stay limited until the power path is fixed (inferred).

**2. Where Voice Lock runs is undecided**

- **What:** Firmware says enrollment cannot run on the headset chip and the chip is near its limit.
- **Tickets:**
  - [HS-178](https://epazz.atlassian.net/browse/HS-178) — Voice Lock enrollment on headset hardware (inferred match)
  - [HS-164](https://epazz.atlassian.net/browse/HS-164) — [POC] SoC Selection, Analysis, and EER Data Collection (still To Do)
  - [HS-166](https://epazz.atlassian.net/browse/HS-166) — Confirm power budget: NXP i.MX 95 at full TSE load
  - [HS-1](https://epazz.atlassian.net/browse/HS-1) — Layer 3 — Inbound TSE (due moved to 2028-02-08)
- **Impact:** A mobile or web app, or a second chip, could be added to the POC plan.

**3. Arc scope proposal is waiting for an answer**

- **What:** The passive Arc was dropped; a smaller plan testing two Arc microphones awaits the manager's reply.
- **Tickets:**
  - [HS-51](https://epazz.atlassian.net/browse/HS-51) — ARC Directional Masking (In Process, proposal 2026-11-10)
  - [HS-79](https://epazz.atlassian.net/browse/HS-79) — Print Arc geometry in PLA (target missed)
  - [HS-78](https://epazz.atlassian.net/browse/HS-78) — [POC] Arc Passive Deflection Feasibility, [TO BE REMOVED] (closed Done)
  - [HS-89](https://epazz.atlassian.net/browse/HS-89) — [POC] Arc Magnetic Latch Force Validation, [TO BE REMOVED] (closed Done)
- **Impact:** Arc mic tests have no dated ticket yet, and closed Arc Stories inflate gate readiness.

## 2. Product Detail

**Purpose:** A headset for call-centre (BPO) agents who wear it for 8–12 hours a shift. It captures the agent's voice cleanly for the Voice Lock speech pipeline (HS-1) and cancels room noise at the ears with ANC, active noise cancelling (HS-2). A clip-on magnetic Arc with two extra microphones is meant to limit how much neighbours overhear (HS-51). It connects over USB-C and Bluetooth (HS-451). The Zoo Meet & cloud Epic (HS-380) was deleted on 2026-11-09/12, and no Epic now owns Zoo Meet integration as a whole.

**Technical requirements by Epic:**

- HS-1 Layer 3 — Inbound TSE (due 2028-02-08, 0% done, variance -155d, To Do)
  - A 4-mic boom array with beam-forming (aiming the mics at the mouth) feeds the Voice Lock TSE model. TSE, target speaker extraction, keeps only the enrolled agent's voice. Targets: EER (equal error rate) ≤1.5% and STI (speech transmission index, a speech-clarity score) ≥0.75 at the call-app input [EE, FW, AC (inferred)]
  - POC: the board bring-up HS-648 (Yocto Linux, ADAU1777 audio codec driver, peripheral tests) is In Process; boom mic capture HS-144, voice detection firmware HS-161 and chip Story HS-164 are still To Do [FW, EE, ME]
  - Platform: CompuLab UCM-iMX95 module (NXP i.MX 95), with everything on the A55 Linux cores (HS-649); chip confirmed in HS-165 [FW, EE]
  - The Epic due date (2028-02-08) is after the MP end, 2027-11-15 (D6, Data Hygiene)
- HS-2 Layer 1 — Outbound(ANC) (due 2027-09-06, 0% done, variance 0d, To Do)
  - Hybrid ANC (outer feed-forward mic plus inner feedback mic): ≥25 dB cancellation from 20 Hz to 1 kHz, with no feedback squeal [EE, FW, AC (inferred)]
  - POC: earcup passive isolation baseline HS-13 (In Process, target ≥15 dB at 100–1000 Hz) and first feedback ANC stability test HS-48 (To Do) [ME, FW]
  - The description says the team has no documented ANC experience and needs a specialist for EVT-2 targets [AC, FW]. The adaptive FxLMS plan is now in question on the current codec (see Engineering discussion)
- HS-3 Power Supply & Battery Management & Fast Charging (due unknown, 17% done, variance n/a, To Do)
  - At least 8 h wireless use at full AI + ANC + audio load; 500 charge cycles at ≥80% capacity [EE]
  - Battery management chip, power rails, battery safety certification, factory battery test; USB-C fast charge 0–50% in ≤20 min (HS-422, not started) [EE, FW, FA (inferred)]
  - Owner Phong.ngoc.nguyen; the due date was removed this week
- HS-4 User Interface (Control & Status) (due 2027-09-06, 0% done, variance -105d, To Do)
  - Three physical controls (power, mute, ANC) and two status LEDs (Voice Lock, battery), usable by touch alone [ME, EE, FW]
  - The LED positions moved to EVT, and EE adds an LED pad on the PCB (HS-301). The ANC touch pad already works on the POC board (HS-652) [ID, EE, FW]
- HS-6 Mechanical Structure (Product Enclosure) (due 2027-09-06, 23% done, variance 0d, In Progress)
  - IP54 (dust and splash resistant); survives a 1.5 m drop; ≤180 g without the Arc [ME]
  - POC headband fit and clamping force are done (HS-580, HS-584, closed 2026-11-12) [ME]
  - The EVT-1 earcup model waits on EE size inputs HS-336 and HS-337 (no owner) [ME, EE]
- HS-47 stop creating trash and assign to me (due 2027-09-06, 0% done, variance n/a, To Do)
  - No description in Jira and no child Stories; nothing to infer.
- HS-51 ARC Directional Masking (NOT THE SAME AS PRO's SOUND MASKING) (due 2027-09-06, 30% done, variance 0d, In Progress)
  - The Jira description is unchanged: a magnetic, detachable Arc on the boom arm plays a masking sound at head height, and passive deflection should cut outward speech by 8–12 dB [ME, AC, EE (inferred)]
  - A 2026-11-10 comment proposes keeping the Epic but only to test the 2 near-field Arc mics (6 mics in total with the boom). The passive approach is dropped (HS-51, Nguyen Trung Tin, 2026-11-10) [ME, AC, EE]
  - The passive POC Stories HS-78, HS-86, HS-89 and HS-127 were all closed as Done [ME]
- HS-142 HEADSET Industrial Design (due 2027-02-08, 25% done, variance +210d, To Do)
  - The headset and Arc shape are developed in three rounds: form model (HS-318 Done), CMF lock (colour, material, finish; HS-320) and pre-DVT geometry sign-off (HS-323) [ID, ME]
  - Six-step design process under HS-326; step 4/6 "Prototyping Shape" (HS-330) is due 2026-11-27 [ID]
- HS-451 Connectivity & Ports (due 2027-09-06, 0% done, variance 0d, To Do)
  - USB-C on the right earcup: USB audio, at least 18 W charging, call-app control (HS-452) [EE, FW, SW, ME]
  - Bluetooth 5.3 multipoint, range ≥20 m (HS-457); 5-pin magnetic connector to the Arc; FCC/CE certification [EE, ME]
- HS-640 Field Test+RC (due 2027-02-08, 0% done, variance +210d, To Do)
  - Field-test plan, end-to-end function, audio quality, consent and voiceprint erasure, battery, firmware update and rollback, and a Go/No-Go report (HS-641 to HS-647, 2027-05-24 → 2027-09-06) [FW, SW (inferred)]

**Engineering discussion (from Jira comments):**

There are 31 HS comments in total, 5 of them new since 2026-11-09 and none between 2026-10-28 and 2026-11-08. I read the comments of every open issue and of the issues closed since the last snapshot. The new board tickets (HS-648 to HS-652) have no comments; their results are in their descriptions, cited below as descriptions.

- **HS-51 Arc Directional Masking**
  - Being discussed: whether to delete the Arc Epic and its Stories (HS-51, Cang Le, 2026-11-09).
  - Thinking so far: keep the Epic but reduce it to what POC can finish. The design sheet has 2 near-field MEMS mics in the Arc, giving 6 mics in total with the 4 on the boom. With the Arc removed, Voice Lock falls back to the 4 boom mics, and the TPS (technical product spec) accepts about 10 dB loss in that mode (HS-51, Nguyen Trung Tin, 2026-11-10).
  - Decided (proposed, not yet approved): stop the passive redirect-and-absorb approach, because "the Halo results so far do not meet the requirement" (HS-51, Nguyen Trung Tin, 2026-11-10). This follows the HS-79 print test (Nguyen Trung Tin, 2026-10-27).
  - Still open: Cang Le's answer to the proposal; which Stories will be removed; who in Acoustic tests the 2 Arc mics, and when. If the 6-mic setup misses the requirement, the Epic is removed next phase. Waiting on Cang Le.
- **HS-2 Outbound (ANC)**
  - Being discussed: getting ANC running on the board's ADAU1777 audio codec (the chip that converts between analogue and digital audio).
  - Thinking so far: ANC tests are "waiting for hardware soldering", and the plan is calibrated ANC on the ADAU1777 (HS-2, Nguyen Binh Minh, 2026-11-09). The audio-path test needs 2 analogue mics and 2 speakers fitted first (HS-219, Nguyen Binh Minh, 2026-11-09).
  - Decided (stated, not approved): adaptive hybrid FxLMS (the self-adjusting ANC algorithm in the Epic plan) "is not possible with current hardware", because the ADAU1777 does not support it, so offline calibration is used instead (HS-221, Nguyen Binh Minh, 2026-11-09).
  - Still open: whether fixed, calibrated ANC can reach the ≥25 dB target; whether EVT needs a different codec or processor (inferred). Waiting on Nguyen Binh Minh.
- **HS-1 Inbound TSE: board bring-up (from descriptions, not comments)**
  - Done: Yocto Linux build for the headset board (HS-649); a new Linux driver for the ADAU1777, written because none existed upstream (HS-650); the image boots on the PCB (HS-651, Done 2026-11-11). All by Phi Tuong.
  - Still open: the power test FAILED, because the PCB will not power up from an external supply without a battery (HS-652, Phi Tuong, created 2026-11-10). No EE comment or ticket answers it.
- **HS-4 User Interface / HS-6 boom mic** (older comments, still open): the LED positions move to EVT (HS-301, Nguyen Trung Tin, 2026-10-19). Acoustic was asked for mic PCB locations and EE for an adjustable-spacing mic PCB (HS-148, Nguyen Trung Tin, 2026-10-12). There is still no answer in Jira.

**Outside Jira comments (stand-up, `transcripts/2026-11-09-standup.md`):** Firmware said voice enrollment cannot be done on the headset itself, so a mobile or web app is needed (`Speaker 1 44:10–44:40`). Minh Nguyen said the i.MX 95 is "up to the limit", so running an outbound voice model as well would need another chip (`1:11:57`). Cang Le asked the tech leads for a proposal next week on where enrollment and the AI models run (`Cang Le 1:15:06`). Khuong Dinh Nguyen will replace the 5-speaker enrollment ticket with clearer tickets (`37:09–38:48`). `comment_signals.json` has 5 entries: HS-51 scope, HS-221 approach, the HS-652 test result, the HS-649 platform and the HS-79 test result.

**Impact traces, Jira HIGH and MEDIUM:**

- **HS-320, HS-323 (HIGH, R6 + R4).** The CMF lock (due 2027-05-24) and the geometry sign-off (due 2027-09-06) sit under Epic HS-142, which ends 2027-02-08, so the Epic really ends 105–210 days later. This is EVT/DVT work, so the POC gate is not affected. ID and ME wait on it. Unchanged since 2026-10-26.
- **HS-641 to HS-647 (HIGH, R4 + R6).** Seven field-test Stories (2027-05-24 → 2027-09-06) sit under Epic HS-640, which ends 2027-02-08, so the Epic slips at least 210 days on paper. This is DVT work with no POC gate impact. Phi Tuong now owns HS-640.
- **HS-648 (MEDIUM, R10).** The rule fires because the board bring-up Story has only firmware Subtasks and no ME/EE/FA one. In fact 3 of its 4 Subtasks are Done (HS-649, HS-650, HS-651). The open one, HS-652, found the board power fault, which needs EE work that has no ticket (source risk). Gate buffer is 80 days. FW waits on EE (inferred).
- **HS-144, HS-161, HS-164, HS-48 (MEDIUM, R2 + R4; R10 on some).** The core POC Stories (boom mic capture, voice detection firmware, chip selection, first ANC test) are 52 days past their start and still To Do. HS-144 and HS-161 are no longer blocked by the board, which has arrived. HS-164 looks finished in practice, since HS-165 is Done and the board uses an i.MX 95 module, but it is still open (see Data Hygiene). HS-48 waits for the ANC mics and speakers to be soldered (HS-219 comment). Each window runs to the gate (2027-02-08), so nothing has slipped yet, but slack is being used up (inferred). FW, EE and ME wait.
- **HS-422, HS-452, HS-457 (MEDIUM).** Fast-charge, USB-C and Bluetooth POC Stories have not started. Now that the board is here they can start, but HS-3 (power) has no due date and HS-452 still names Zoo Meet, whose Epic was deleted.
- **HS-326 (MEDIUM, R10).** The form-model Story is In Process (owner Bui Minh Tuan). Its step HS-330 is due 2026-11-27. No gate threat.
- **Epics HS-1, HS-2, HS-3, HS-4, HS-142, HS-451, HS-640 (MEDIUM, R2).** Start dates have passed and the status is still To Do, although four of them were given owners this week. This is a status issue with no gate impact of its own.

**Impact traces, source risks:**

- **Voice Lock compute and enrollment (MEDIUM; S1).** If enrollment moves to an app, the Headset needs software and app tickets that do not exist (HS-178 assumes on-device enrollment). If an outbound model is added, the i.MX 95 may not have the capacity (`2026-11-09-standup.md · Minh Nguyen 1:11:57`). The proposal is due at next week's tech-lead meeting. POC work under HS-1 continues on the current board in the meantime (inferred).
- **Arc scope (MEDIUM; S1).** The proposal (HS-51, 2026-11-10) waits for Cang Le. Until it is answered, the Arc 2-mic validation has no Story or date, and the closed "[TO BE REMOVED]" Stories still count toward POC readiness.
- **EE tickets missing (MEDIUM; S1).** 24 days after EE said it would create Headset tickets (`2026-10-19-biweekly.md · Pham Nguyen Ngoc 22:25`), the board has arrived with a power fault and there is still no EE ticket or owner. The EE size inputs HS-130, HS-131, HS-336 and HS-337 are unowned, so the earcup model (HS-128 → HS-6) may need rework (inferred).
- **Mechanical capacity (MEDIUM; S1).** Nguyen Trung Tin owns HS-13 (In Process), HS-51 and the Arc re-scope, and also supports the Pro and Beacon. He was out of office on the stand-up day. No split of hours has been agreed.
- **Headset PCBs (closed).** Delivered by the 2026-11-09 stand-up, and the board boots (HS-651). Earlier it was held at the supplier over a ~$35 charge for a wrong board option. At the stand-up, Pham Nguyen Ngoc said she did not think the board was wrong but that it had not been tested yet (`2026-11-09-standup.md · Pham Nguyen Ngoc 24:28`).

## 5. Recommendations for TM

1. **Give the board power fault an EE owner this week.** Open an EE Subtask under HS-648 for "PCB does not power up without a battery" (found in HS-652), with a due date, so FW testing on the only board can continue. Assign HS-130, HS-131, HS-336 and HS-337 at the same time. Owners: Pham Nguyen Ngoc (EE lead), Phong.ngoc.nguyen (HS-3 owner).
2. **Record where Voice Lock runs.** At next week's tech-lead meeting, decide whether enrollment and the voice models run on the i.MX 95 or in a mobile/web app, and whether the Pro/Beacon web app also serves the Headset. Write the decision on HS-1 and replace HS-178 with the tickets that follow from it. Close HS-164, since the chip is confirmed in HS-165, and finish the power budget HS-166. Owners: Cang Le, Khuong Dinh Nguyen (HS-1), Minh Nguyen, Phi Tuong (HS-166), and Tien (named at `2026-11-09-standup.md · 1:15:06`).
3. **Answer the Arc proposal on HS-51.** Approve or reject Nguyen Trung Tin's 2026-11-10 plan. Then delete, rather than close as Done, the "[TO BE REMOVED]" Stories (HS-78, HS-86, HS-89, HS-127) so POC readiness is not overstated. Add a dated Story for the 2-mic Arc test with an Acoustic owner. Owners: Cang Le, Nguyen Trung Tin.
4. **Finish the ticket re-evaluation with owners and dates.** Now that the board is in, assign and start HS-144 (boom mic capture) and HS-161 (voice detection firmware). Plan HS-48 (first ANC test) once the ANC mics and speakers are soldered (HS-219). Move HS-1's due date (2028-02-08) back inside the MP window (ends 2027-11-15), give HS-3 a due date, and confirm whether Zoo Meet work (HS-452, HS-457) is still in scope now that HS-380 is deleted. Owners: Cang Le, KhuongNguyen (HS-1), Nguyen Binh Minh (HS-2), Phong.ngoc.nguyen (HS-3).
5. **Clear POC Sprint 3.** 31 open tickets still sit in Sprint 3, which ended 2026-11-01, including HS-164, HS-48, HS-422 and HS-166. At the stand-up, Cang Le asked owners to take unworked tickets out of sprints (`2026-11-09-standup.md · Cang Le 9:42`). Move each one to Sprint 4 or remove it from the sprint. Owners: each ticket's owner, with Cang Le for the 27 unassigned ones.

## 6. Data Gaps & Assumptions

- Snapshot `data/ZenaQuiet-Headset/2026-11-12.json`: 521 issues; field IDs: start date `customfield_10015`, sprint `customfield_10020`.
- **Jira pull:** the JQL `project = HS AND issuetype in (Epic, Story, Sub-task, Subtask) ORDER BY key ASC` returned 521 (count query), down from 556 on 2026-11-09 (40 deleted, 5 added). I retrieved 6 pages (HS-1 → HS-652) using `nextPageToken` and saved them unchanged. `normalize` found 521 unique issues, equal to the JQL total: 10 Epics, 97 Stories, 414 Subtasks. Every issue's comments came back complete (31 in total), so no `WORK/comments/` files were needed. Story → Epic and Subtask → Story use `parent`. Field IDs `customfield_10015` (start) and `customfield_10020` (sprint) were confirmed in the data.
- **Deleted tickets:** the 40 removed keys (HS-162, HS-167, HS-380 to HS-417) are gone from Jira, so why each was removed cannot be checked. The only stated reason is the stand-up remark that AI-drafted tickets were never reviewed (`2026-11-09-standup.md · Duy Cong Nguyen 25:23`).
